  - Workflows
    - [Workflows](/docs/asset-lifecycle/workflows): Workflows documentation
    - [Working with BOMs](/docs/asset-lifecycle/workflows/bills-of-materials): A bill of materials (BOM) represents a collection of products (devices, modules, cables, etc.) to be ordered for installation.
    - [Installing Objects](/docs/asset-lifecycle/workflows/installation): Objects in NetBox can be recorded as having been sourced via a particular PO/shipment or from a spares pool. Note that this supported only for objects which ...
    - [Working with Purchase Orders](/docs/asset-lifecycle/workflows/purchase-orders): Purchase orders represent a collection of products to be ordered from a vendor, generated from a bill of materials.
    - [Working with Shipments](/docs/asset-lifecycle/workflows/shipments): Once a PO has been ordered, you can start tracking shipments from the vendor. This will typically occur when a vendor advises you of a shipment pertaining to...
    - [Spares Management](/docs/asset-lifecycle/workflows/spares-management): Equipment that is kept on standby for potential future use is managed by defining spares pools.

  - Models
    - [Models](/docs/branching/models): Models documentation
    - [Branches](/docs/branching/models/branch): A branch represents a divergent state of the NetBox database, isolated within a dedicated PostgreSQL schema. Changes made within a branch do not affect main ...
    - [Branch Events](/docs/branching/models/branchevent): Branch lifecycle operations (provisioning, syncing, merging, etc.) are recorded as branch events. The list of events associated with a branch serves as its o...
    - [Change Diffs](/docs/branching/models/changediff): A change diff summarizes the net effect of all changes to a particular NetBox object within a branch. It simplifies the process of reviewing changes within a...
    - [Object Changes](/docs/branching/models/objectchange): This model serves as a proxy for NetBox's native ObjectChange model.

    - [Syncing & Merging Changes](/docs/branching/using-branches/syncing-merging): Synchronizing a branch replicates all recent changes from main into the branch. These changes can be reviewed under the Changes Behind tab of the branch view.

    - [Application Registry](/docs/netbox/development/application-registry): The registry is an in-memory data structure which houses various application-wide parameters, such as the list of enabled plugins. It is not exposed to the u...
    - [User Preferences](/docs/netbox/development/user-preferences): The users.UserConfig model holds individual preferences for each user in the form of JSON data. This page serves as a manifest of all recognized user prefere...
    - [Web UI Development](/docs/netbox/development/web-ui): Most static resources for the NetBox UI are housed within the netbox/project-static/ directory.

    - [Translations](/docs/netbox/development/translations): NetBox coordinates all translation work using the Transifex platform. Signing up for a Transifex account is free.

      - [Web UI Development](/docs/netbox/v4.0/development/web-ui): Most static resources for the NetBox UI are housed within the netbox/project-static/ directory.
      - [Internationalization](/docs/netbox/v4.0/development/internationalization): Beginning with NetBox v4.0, NetBox will leverage Django's automatic translation to support languages other than English. This page details the areas of the p...
      - [Translations](/docs/netbox/v4.0/development/translations): NetBox coordinates all translation work using the Transifex platform. Signing up for a Transifex account is free.
